To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [253] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究测度空间中的优化问题，该问题以到给定先验测度的传输距离作为正则化项。利用弱-star二阶次微分理论推导无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，该条件在目标函数光滑部分和Kantorovich势的额外假设下与二次增长等价。文中计算了弱-star二阶次微分并证明了弱-star上表微分性，最后将结果应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测度空间中的优化问题广泛出现在最优传输、最优控制等领域，其中以传输距离作为正则化项的模型在实际应用中日益普遍。现有理论主要用于一阶最优性条件的推导，而二阶最优性条件对理解问题的局部结构、设计高效的数值算法至关重要，因此需要建立该框架下的二阶变分分析理论。

Method: 本文采用弱-star二阶次微分（weak-$\star$ second subdifferential）理论来刻画测度空间中带传输距离正则项优化问题的二阶最优性条件。具体步骤包括：利用对偶传输问题（Kantorovich对偶）求解Kantorovich势；计算弱-star二阶次微分的显式表达式；证明弱-star上表微分性（weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability）；在目标函数光滑部分和Kantorovich势的适当假设下建立与二次增长的等价关系。

Result: 得到了无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，即在适当的假设下，弱-star二阶次微分的正定性条件与二次增长性质等价。同时完成了弱-star二阶次微分的显式计算，并证明了弱-star上表微分性，为测度空间中最优控制问题的二阶分析提供了完整的理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文建立了测度空间中带传输距离正则化优化问题的完整二阶变分分析框架，包括二阶最优性条件、弱-star二阶次微分的计算及上表微分性证明。该理论被成功应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题，为这些问题的理论分析和算法设计奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [254] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限个proper、下半连续凸函数的邻近算子方程组$(\forall i \in I) \pr{f_i}(x) = p_i$的精确解的存在性与唯一性，建立了近似解存在的充要条件，提出了逆邻近性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广并给出多种刻画，最后应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的投影可行性问题可自然推广为邻近算子方程组问题。该研究旨在理解这类广义系统的可解性结构，并将逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）推广到逆邻近性质（IPP），以服务优化与反问题领域。

Method: 在实Hilbert空间框架下，利用凸分析的邻近算子理论和投影方法的推广，给出系统精确解/近似解存在性的充要条件，并在此基础上引入IPP并推导其多种等价刻画，最后将理论结果应用于可行性问题与信号恢复问题。

Result: 建立了邻近算子方程组精确解的存在性和唯一性条件，给出了近似解存在性的充要条件，提出了逆邻近性质（IPP）并得到多种等价刻画，相关结果在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中得到了有效应用。

Conclusion: 该研究将经典投影问题系统性地推广到邻近算子方程组，建立了完备的可解性理论框架，为Hilbert空间中的优化与逆问题提供了新的理论工具和应用途径。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [255] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策图（DD）的割平面生成方法用于二元多项式优化（BPO）。该方法针对超图多线性集，对任意选定的支持（support）生成局部多线性多面体的面定义割并提升回原问题，采用新型紧凑DD编码（仅表示顶点变量，超边变量隐式编码在状态中）。该编码还带来扩展公式、通过有效反链的宽度刻画（发现新的多项式可解超图类）以及生成割的面性证书。实验表明该方法在根节点实现更大间隙闭合、需更少割，并显著加速分支定界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的多线性集强不等式（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）都是针对固定支持模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性和系统性。本文旨在提出一种自动化的割平面生成方法，对任意支持都能分离面定义割，克服手工推导的局限。

Method: 提出基于决策图（DD）的割平面生成框架：(a) 设计基于递归公式的新型紧凑DD编码，仅表示顶点变量，超边变量隐式编码在状态中；(b) 对任意选定支持，在局部多线性多面体中分离面定义割并提升回原问题；(c) 从DD编码获得扩展公式、宽度刻画（通过有效反链）和面性证书；(d) 探索三种支持策略（复用、扩展、划分局部结构为section超图）用于割生成。

Result: 实证结果显示DD方法在根节点实现更大的间隙闭合，所需割数量更少，并显著加速分支定界过程，优于现有割平面方法。

Conclusion: 基于DD的割平面生成方法为BPO提供了一种通用、自动化的强割生成框架，不仅优于手工推导的经典割族，还通过宽度刻画发现了新的多项式可解超图类，具有理论和实践双重价值。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>
